However, their performance remains limited in heterophilous graphs, where nodes with different class labels are more likely to be connected. In particular, current GNNs derived from graph convolutional networks cannot capture higher-order class label connectivity, which is frequently observed in real-world heterophilous graphs. To address this issue, we propose a novel classifier, Label Context Classifier (LCC), designed to capture higher-order class label connectivity in directed graphs. LCC estimates the class label of a target node by leveraging label context embeddings that are generated through four distinct types of walks. In addition, our approach allows the integration of LCC and any GNN by adaptively learning their importance. Experimental results demonstrate that GNNs integrated with LCC outperform SOTA methods and the label context embeddings improve the node classification performance in heterophilous directed graphs.

</details>


### [75] [$α$-PFN: Fast Entropy Search via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07134)
*Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Steven Adriaensen,Tom Viering,Carl Hvarfner,Samuel Müller,Frank Hutter,Eytan Bakshy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段摊销策略，利用先验数据拟合网络（PFNs）来近似熵搜索获取函数，实现单次前向传播即可完成信息增益的预测，在保持竞争力的同时实现50倍以上的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于信息论的获取函数（如熵搜索）虽然在贝叶斯优化中提供了原则性的探索-利用框架，但其实际实现依赖于复杂且缓慢的蒙特卡洛近似，这种复杂性引入数值误差且需要专门的手工实现。

Method: 提出两阶段摊销策略：第一阶段训练PFN使其以最优值信息为条件；第二阶段训练α-PFN通过第一阶段PFN测量的信息增益来预测期望信息增益，用单次前向传播替代复杂的启发式近似。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中与最先进的熵搜索实现竞争力相当，同时在不同熵搜索变体上实现超过50倍的加速。

Conclusion: α-PFN提供了一种灵活的学习近似方法，能够快速且可扩展地进行获取函数评估，替代了复杂的启发式近似。

Abstract: Information-theoretic acquisition functions such as Entropy Search (ES) offer a principled exploration-exploitation framework for Bayesian optimization (BO). However, their practical implementation relies on complicated and slow approximations, i.e., a Monte Carlo estimation of the information gain. This complexity can introduce numerical errors and requires specialized, hand-crafted implementations. We propose a two-stage amortization strategy that learns to approximate entropy search-based acquisition functions using Prior-data Fitted Networks (PFNs) in a single forward pass. A first PFN is trained to be conditioned on information about the optima; second, the $α$-PFN is trained to predict the expected information gain by training on information gains measured with the first PFN. The $α$-PFN offers a flexible learned approximation, which replaces the complex heuristic approximations with a single forward pass per candidate, enabling rapid and extensible acquisition evaluation. Empirically, our approach is competitive with state-of-the-art entropy search implementations on synthetic and real-world benchmarks, while accelerating the different entropy search variants across all our experiments, with speed ups over 50x. Source code: https://github.com/automl/AlphaPFN.

</details>


### [76] [Sparse Subspace-to-Expert Sharing for Task-Agnostic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07500)
*Fatema Siddika,Md Anwar Hossen,Tanwi Mallick,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SETA通过自适应稀疏子空间分解为任务特定专家和共享专家，解决了LLM持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性冲突，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM持续学习面临可塑性-稳定性困境，现有方法对所有参数统一处理，无法区分任务特定知识与共享能力，导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出SETA框架，通过自适应稀疏子空间分解为任务特定专家模块（隔离任务特定模式）和共享专家模块（捕获通用特征），采用自适应弹性锚定和路由感知正则化在权重和路由层面保护共享知识，统一门控网络在推理时自动检索正确的专家组合。

Result: 在多个领域特定基准上相比SOTA持续学习基线取得竞争性或更优的总体性能，尤其在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上展现出更强的早期任务知识保留能力和反向迁移能力。

Conclusion: SETA通过稀疏专家混合架构有效解决了持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性矛盾，实现了知识隔离与共享的平衡。

Abstract: Continual learning in Large Language Models (LLMs) is hindered by the plasticity-stability dilemma, where acquiring new capabilities often leads to catastrophic forgetting of previous knowledge. Existing methods typically treat parameters uniformly, failing to distinguish between specific task knowledge and shared capabilities. We introduce Mixture of Sparse Experts for Task Agnostic Continual Learning (SETA), a framework that resolves the plasticity-stability conflict through adaptive sparse subspace decomposition into task-specific expert modules. Unlike standard updates, where tasks compete for the same parameters, SETA separates knowledge into unique experts, designed to isolate task-specific patterns, and shared experts, responsible for capturing common features. This structure is maintained through adaptive elastic anchoring and a routing-aware regularization that jointly protect shared knowledge at both the weight and routing levels and enable a unified gating network to automatically retrieve the correct expert combination during inference. Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.

</details>


### [77] [Explaining Unsupervised Disease Staging in Huntington's Disease: Insights into Model Representations and Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07135)
*Lubna Mahmoud Abu Zohair,Hind Zantout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在已有的无监督机器学习疾病分期框架基础上，通过降维可视化、显著性图和SHAP等方法进行可解释性分析，证实了模型学到的特征表征和疾病阶段与亨廷顿舞蹈症的临床进展模式高度一致，揭示了从早期认知-运动障碍到严重功能依赖的分层结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亨廷顿舞蹈症是一种进行性神经退行性疾病，准确刻画疾病进展对改善患者预后和生活质量至关重要。尽管无监督机器学习能从纵向数据中挖掘疾病进展轨迹和潜在阶段，但其可解释性有限，限制了临床信任和转化应用。

Method: 对已有的无监督ML疾病分期框架进行可解释性分析：1) 将学习到的特征表示投影到低维空间评估聚类是否与临床指标进展对齐；2) 使用显著性图识别随时间对嵌入贡献最强的临床特征；3) 训练替代分类器并应用SHAP量化特征对聚类分配的重要性，分析驱动阶段间转换的临床变量。

Result: 可解释性分析表明：学习到的嵌入捕获了有临床意义的疾病结构，与既定运动和功能严重程度评分一致，聚类间呈现进行性恶化趋势。SHAP揭示了从早期认知-运动障碍到严重功能依赖的疾病阶段分层，与已知临床进展模式一致，同时揭示了阶段内变异性。

Conclusion: 通过可解释性分析，验证了无监督机器学习框架在HD疾病进展建模中的临床有效性，增强了模型的透明度和可信度，有助于推动该框架向临床转化应用。

Abstract: Huntington's disease (HD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder that affects motor, cognitive, and behavioral functions, where accurate characterization of disease progression remains essential to improve patient outcome and quality of life. Unsupervised machine learning (ML) approaches have demonstrated the ability to uncover disease progression trajectories and meaningful latent stages from longitudinal data; however, their limited interpretability restricts clinical trust and translation. We extend a previously proposed ML-based disease staging framework by applying an explainability analysis to the extracted feature representations and discovered disease stages. Applied to the Enroll-HD dataset, we first project the learned representations into a lower-dimensional space to intuitively assess whether the resulting clusters align with the progression of established clinical measures. We then use saliency maps to identify the clinical features that most strongly contribute to the learned embeddings over time. Finally, we train a surrogate classifier and apply SHAP to quantify feature importance for cluster assignments and to analyze which clinical variables drive transitions between disease stages. The explainability analysis indicates that the learned embeddings capture clinically meaningful disease structure, aligning with established motor and functional severity scores and exhibiting progressive deterioration across clusters. Within this analysis, SHAP reveals a stratification of disease stages, ranging from early cognitive-motor impairment to severe functional dependency, consistent with known clinical progression patterns, while also highlighting intra-stage variability.

</details>


### [78] [Decision-Aware Evaluation of Physics-Informed Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07146)
*Daniel Cieślak,Andrzej Czyżewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出pinn-gym基准测试，用于评估物理信息机器学习（PIML）代理模型在材料约束晶格设计中的决策性能，而不仅仅是曲线拟合精度。结果表明低nRMSE不足以识别有用的设计选择，物理信息损失会改变不同指标间的权衡而非单调提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理信息机器学习评估仅关注曲线误差，但工程实际应用依赖于下游决策（如候选排序、避免不可行设计、限制遗憾值）。现有评估方式与工程需求脱节，需要建立以决策为导向的评估框架。

Method: 构建pinn-gym开源基准，包括：(1) 透明降阶碰撞冲击Oracle；(2) 五种可打印聚合物材料卡；(3) 无量纲力响应目标；(4) 涵盖曲线保真度、物理可行性、top-k检索和质量遗憾值的多维度评估协议。实验覆盖单材料、混合材料和跨材料三种设置。

Result: 低nRMSE不足以识别有用的设计选择；物理信息损失会改变不同指标间的权衡关系而非单调提升所有指标；无量纲化处理改善可比性但不使迁移具有对称性。

Conclusion: pinn-gym提供了可复现的测试平台，用于将PIML代理模型评估为决策系统而非单纯的曲线预测器。该基准并非认证材料模型，而是在发布的Oracle、候选生成器和材料卡框架内提供标准化评估环境。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning is often assessed by curve error, although engineering use depends on downstream decisions: ranking candidates, avoiding infeasible designs and limiting regret. We introduce pinn-gym, an open benchmark for material-conditioned lattice design that couples a transparent reduced-order crush-and-impact oracle with five printable polymer cards, dimensionless force-response targets and a protocol spanning curve fidelity, physical admissibility, top-k retrieval and mass regret.
  Across per-material, pooled and cross-material settings, low nRMSE is frequently insufficient to identify useful design selections. Physics-informed losses alter trade-offs rather than monotonically improving all metrics, and dimensionless conditioning improves comparability without making transfer symmetric. The benchmark is not a certified material model; within the released oracle, candidate generator and material cards, pinn-gym provides a reproducible testbed for evaluating PIML surrogates as decision systems rather than curve predictors alone.

</details>


### [79] [Geodesics of Dynamic Graphs for Regime Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07151)
*William Cappelletti,Étienne Voutaz,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出在图空间中检测动态网络演化状态变化的方法，通过将状态定义为时序图沿测地线的轨迹，利用图回归测量累积距离来识别轨迹或速度的显著漂移，在实验和COVID-19移动数据中 outperforms 现有变点检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态网络变点检测假设状态间 abrupt 切换，但现实网络（如社交网络或物理系统）多呈现 continuous evolution，现有方法无法处理这一场景。

Method: 将"状态"定义为时序图在恰当定义的图空间中沿测地线的轨迹；将"状态变化"定义为向新轨迹的显著漂移或节奏变化；利用图回归方法度量观测图序列与其端点间估计测地线的累积距离，并结合变点检测算法。

Result: 在轨迹变化和速度变化的动态网络实验中 outperforms 现有最先进变点检测模型；在 COVID-19 期间移动数据分析中，检测到的变点比基线方法更符合外部事件。

Conclusion: 首次在图空间中建模和检测演化状态之间的变化，为分析复杂时序图数据提供了更真实且有力的工具。

Abstract: Traditional change point detection in dynamic networks assumes abrupt transitions between stationary states, overlooking scenarios of continuous evolution which arise in most real-world applications, such as social networks or physical systems. We address this gap by formally defining regimes as periods of coherent dynamics in temporal graphs, which we characterize as trajectories along geodesics in a suitably defined graph space. This original perspective allows us to define regime changes as significant drifts in dynamics, either toward new trajectories or with pace changes. We leverage graph regression methods to measure the cumulative distance of sequences of observed graphs from the estimated geodesics between their endpoints, in the relevant graph space, which we can combine with change point detection algorithms. We present experiments on dynamic networks, with changing trajectories and varying speeds, in which we outperform state of the art change point detection models. Then, we analyse mobility data during the Covid-19 pandemic, and show that our assumptions on regular network evolution lead to change points that are more aligned to external events compared to the outcomes of baseline methods. Our work is the first to model and detect changes between evolving regimes in graph space, providing a realistic and powerful tool for analyzing complex temporal graph data.

</details>


### [80] [Structure-Preserving Correction Learning for Sparse Bayesian Inference in Brain Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07196)
*Marco Morik,Xiao Ruiting,Shinichi Nakajima,Stefan Haufe,Ismail Huseynov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过展开经典联合超参数学习求解器为可训练神经网络架构，在学习结构化修正项的同时保留原始贝叶斯更新机制的透明性和可解释性，实验表明该方法在M/EEG脑成像中提升了重建性能与收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稀疏Type-II贝叶斯方法虽能联合估计源和噪声超参数，但依赖固定的迭代更新规则，其动态无法从数据中自适应调整，限制了重建性能的提升。

Method: 将经典联合超参数学习求解器展开为可训练神经网络架构，层结构与原始迭代对应；初始化时精确恢复经典求解器，随后通过渐进式修正学习机制（可学习偏置、自适应MLP、基于注意力的上下文精炼）增强表达能力。

Result: 学习后的修正变体在重建性能和收敛行为上均优于基线展开求解器，同时保持了算法的透明性。

Conclusion: 结构化修正学习能在不替代原有基于模型的推理机制的前提下，通过可学习的修正项提升经验重建性能，是一种兼顾可解释性与性能提升的有效范式。

Abstract: Classical sparse Type-II Bayesian methods for M/EEG brain imaging support joint estimation of source and noise hyperparameters, but rely on fixed iterative update rules. Although these updates are principled and interpretable, their dynamics cannot be adapted from data. We propose to learn the update mechanism itself while preserving the underlying Bayesian structure by unfolding a classical joint hyperparameter-learning solver into a trainable neural architecture whose layers mirror the original iterations. The resulting framework is initialized to recover the classical solver exactly before training and is enriched through progressively more expressive correction-learning mechanisms, ranging from learnable biases to adaptive MLP and attention-based contextual refinements. In this way, training does not replace Bayesian inference with a black-box predictor, but instead learns structured correction terms while retaining the interpretability and model-based character of the original update dynamics. Structured correction learning therefore aims to improve empirical reconstruction performance without replacing the original model-based inference mechanism. Experimental results show that the learned correction variants improve reconstruction performance and convergence behavior over the baseline unfolded solver while preserving its algorithmic transparency.

</details>


### [81] [Generative Molecular Morphing for Flexible-Size Design via Unbalanced Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07239)
*Malte Franke,Stefan P. Schmid,Zarko Ivkovic,Kjell Jorner,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Morph是一种基于几何图的可变尺寸3D分子生成模型，通过动态调整原子数量，在保持与固定尺寸模型相当性能的同时，实现了更灵活的条件/无条件生成和更好的性质调控能力，尤其在分布外生成方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流模型固定原子数量，无法有效捕捉分子性质与分子大小之间的内在关联，限制了模型在性质优化方向上的可引导性。

Method: 提出了Morph——一个基于几何图的可变尺寸生成模型，支持条件和非条件3D分子设计，能够动态调整分子大小并无缝整合现有结构先验（如骨架）。

Result: Morph在性能上媲美当前最先进的固定尺寸模型，同时提供了无与伦比的采样灵活性；在先前模型失败的区域成功实现了分布外生成。

Conclusion: 可变尺寸生成策略有效解决了分子性质与大小联合建模的难题，为增强分子设计的生成建模开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The success of generative molecular design hinges on a model's steerability toward high-reward samples. Because many molecular properties are intrinsically linked to molecular size, accurately capturing the joint distribution of properties and the number of atoms is essential. However, current diffusion and flow-based models fix the number of atoms, which ultimately limits their ability to navigate this complex relationship. To address this, we introduce Morph, a flexible-size generative model for conditional and unconditional 3D molecular design based on geometric graphs. By dynamically adapting size, Morph can seamlessly integrate existing structural priors, like scaffolds, and significantly enhances property steering. We show that Morph matches current fixed-size state-of-the-art models while offering the benefit of unparalleled sampling flexibility. We demonstrate out-of-distribution generation in regimes where previous models fail, paving the way for enhanced generative modeling for molecular design.

</details>


### [82] [A Held-Out Transition-Pair Falsifier for Long-Horizon Non-Abelian State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07254)
*Jeonghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于有限非阿贝尔群状态跟踪的留出过渡对伪造协议，并证明显式投影非交换状态组合的循环模型在长达百万级token的评估范围内实现了完美的状态预测，而标准序列模型（如GRU、状态空间模型等）在该协议下性能接近基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态跟踪揭示了序列模型的一个根本局限：相关信号往往不是观测token的摘要，而是通过非交换变换演化的有序潜在状态。现有模型难以处理长程非交换群状态跟踪问题。

Method: 提出了留出过渡对伪造协议（held-out transition-pair falsifier），禁止训练中出现特定有序生成器对，但要求评估时识别相同的局部模式。在S₃×S₃基准上，使用投影循环状态模型（projected recurrent state model）仅基于长度8的序列训练，并通过投影匹配的基线对比（bag、GRU、结构化状态空间模型）进行验证。

Result: 投影循环状态模型在评估视野达1,048,576个token时仍能产生无错误的最终状态预测（五个种子下250/250完美预测）。而匹配的本地读出基线（bag、GRU、单配置结构化SSM）以及投影匹配的基线均接近随机水平。硬投影与低同态误差、低状态一致性漂移和非平凡交换子分离相关，而软化投影则导致最终状态准确率崩溃。

Conclusion: 在受控有限群伪造协议范围内，显式投影非交换状态组合作为长程隐藏状态跟踪的有用归纳偏置。

Abstract: State tracking exposes a sharp limitation of sequence models: the relevant signal is often not a summary of observed tokens, but an ordered latent state that evolves through non-commutative transformations. We introduce a held-out transition-pair falsifier for finite non-Abelian group tracking. The protocol forbids selected ordered generator pairs during training and requires the same local patterns during evaluation, blocking one direct local-transition memorization pathway. In a controlled $S_3 \times S_3$ benchmark, a projected recurrent state model trained only on length-8 sequences produces error-free final-state predictions (perfect 250/250 per horizon) through evaluation horizons up to 1,048,576 tokens across five seeds. Matched native-readout baselines, including bag, GRU, and a single-configuration structured state-space model, remain near floor under the same protocol. Projection-matched GRU, structured SSM, and bag baselines equipped with analogous finite-group prototype readouts also remain near chance under the same split. Mechanism diagnostics show that hard projection coincides with low homomorphism error, low state-consistency drift, and non-trivial commutator separation, while softened projection collapses final-state accuracy. Clean-split audits verify zero verbatim reduced-word overlap and zero structural-template overlap between training and evaluation partitions. The evidence is scoped to this controlled finite-group falsifier rather than to a general architecture ranking. Within that regime, explicit projected non-commutative state composition acts as a useful inductive bias for long-horizon hidden-state tracking.

</details>


### [83] [Closed-Form Spectral Regularization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07289)
*Yongxian Wei,Runxi Cheng,Xingxuan Zhang,Li Shen,Chun Yuan,Peng Cui,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多任务模型合并中的迭代求解器主要起隐式谱正则化作用而非优化作用，并据此提出基于谱滤波的闭式合并方法SWUDI及其自适应变体SWUDI-A，在多个基准上达到或超越SOTA性能，同时将耗时减少28-72倍、GPU显存降低最多50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的模型合并方法将合并表述为逐层二次干扰最小化问题，虽存在闭式伪逆解，但实际性能远不如数百次梯度下降迭代。作者希望理解迭代求解器为何有效，并设计更高效、无需迭代的合并方法。

Method: 1) 形式化多任务模型合并为含噪线性逆问题；2) 提出谱滤波估计器，每个方向使用独立滤波器；3) 设计SWUDI：软指数滤波器（匹配梯度下降轨迹）+ 硬Top-K截断（抑制小特征值方向的噪声放大）；4) 提出SWUDI-A：用逐层秩规则替代全局秩超参数；5) 方法只需每线性层一次对称特征分解，无需训练数据或优化器状态。

Result: 在4个通用基准和1个多模态合并基准（含VQA、几何、图表、OCR、Grounding、模态合并）上，SWUDI/SWUDI-A匹配或超越SOTA合并方法；耗时减少28-72倍，峰值GPU显存降低最多50%。

Conclusion: 迭代求解器在模型合并中的成功并非源于优化能力，而是因其隐式谱正则化效应。基于此洞察设计的闭式谱滤波方法可大幅提升合并效率，同时保持甚至提升性能，为大规模多任务模型合并提供了实用的高效方案。

Abstract: Model merging combines several independently fine-tuned experts into a single multi-task model without any training data, reducing the storage, serving, and decentralized-development costs of large foundation models. State-of-the-art merging methods formulate merging as a layer-wise quadratic interference minimization problem. Although this problem admits an exact closed-form pseudoinverse solution, that solution underperforms hundreds of iterations of gradient descent in practice. The iterative loop dominates the cost of the pipeline, yet its effectiveness has remained unexplained. We revisit this regime and show that the iterative solver does not primarily act as an optimizer; rather, it serves as an implicit spectral regularizer for an ill-posed normal equation, where small-eigenvalue directions of the per-layer interference operator amplify proxy noise. Building on this finding, we formalize multi-task model merging as a noisy linear inverse problem and propose a spectral filtering estimator parameterized by a per-direction filter. We instantiate this estimator with SWUDI, a closed-form method that combines a soft exponential filter, which matches the gradient-flow trajectory of iterative descent, with a hard top-K truncation that suppresses noise-amplifying small-eigenvalue directions. Furthermore, we propose SWUDI-A, an adaptive variant that replaces the global rank hyperparameter with per-layer rank rules, further improving robustness across architectures. Both variants share a single symmetric eigendecomposition per linear layer and require no training data or optimizer state. Across four general benchmarks and a multimodal merging benchmark spanning VQA, Geometry, Chart, OCR, Grounding, and modality merging, our proposed spectral solvers match or outperform state-of-the-art merging methods. Crucially, they reduce wall-clock time by 28-72x and peak GPU memory by up to 50%.

</details>


### [84] [Trio: Learning Time-Series Forecasting with Temporal-Spatial-Sample Attention and Structural Causal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07291)
*Tao Chen,Yexu Zhou,Zhi Gong,Hengwei He,Hongda Li,Zhewei Chen,Dongjing Wang,Xin Zhang,Decheng Liu,Chunlei Peng,Zheng Chen,Wenyue Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Trio架构，基于时间-空间-样本三重注意力机制，结合TS-SCM生成器构建结构化合成任务，实现多变量时间序列预测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Prior-Data Fitted Networks（PFNs）依赖表格先验，无法天然捕捉时间顺序、动态滞后和重复历史模式，难以直接迁移到时间序列预测任务中。

Method: 提出Trio架构：时间注意力捕获窗口内动态，空间注意力建模跨变量依赖，样本注意力检索历史lookback-future对辅助预测。并引入TS-SCM生成器创建含动态滞后、跨变量交互、噪声、反馈和分布漂移的结构化合成任务。

Result: 在合成数据、工业数据和公开基准上，Trio架构均提升了预测性能。零样本实验表明TS-SCM生成的任务可提供有用结构先验，但完全通用PFN式时间序列预测仍是开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过显式组织和重用历史输入-输出样例可有效提升时间序列预测，但构建全通用PFN式预测器仍具挑战。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series forecasting requires models to reason over temporal dynamics, cross-variable dependencies, and historical input-output correspondences. Recent Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) suggest that synthetic tasks can be useful for learning transferable inference behavior. However, directly transferring this paradigm to time-series forecasting remains difficult, since temporal order, dynamic lags, and recurring historical patterns are not naturally captured by ordinary tabular priors. Motivated by this observation, we propose Trio, a sample-aware time-series forecasting architecture based on Temporal-Spatial-Sample attention. Temporal attention captures within-window dynamics, spatial attention models inter-variable dependencies, and sample attention retrieves relevant historical lookback-future pairs to guide the current prediction. Rather than claiming a fully general PFN-style forecaster, our goal is to study how historical input-output examples can be explicitly organized and reused within a forecasting model. We further introduce a Time-Series Structural Causal Model (TS-SCM) generator to create structured synthetic forecasting tasks with dynamic lags, cross-variable interactions, noise, feedback, and distributional drift. Experiments on synthetic, industrial, and public benchmarks show that the proposed architecture improves forecasting performance. Exploratory zero-shot experiments further suggest that TS-SCM-generated tasks may provide useful structural priors, while fully general PFN-style time-series forecasting remains an open problem.

</details>


### [85] [Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence: Explanatory Insufficiency as a Driver of Representation Learning and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07303)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Elsa Raynal,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征涌现的自举理论（TBER），认为新表征的产生并非仅由数据和算力驱动，更源于现有表征的解释力不足。当表征的描述域被超越但尚未完全错误时，即出现解释性不充分，这正是表征跃迁的积极信号。该过程遵循递归序列：观察→异常→不充分→新表征→新观察，包含五个阶段：稳定观察、异常检测、解释性不充分识别、表征涌现、暂态稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注在选定表征框架后如何优化表征，而忽略了一个关键问题：何时需要创造出新的表征层次。这种对表征创新触发机制的研究缺失，使得我们无法理解从手工特征到学习嵌入、潜在空间、基础模型、世界模型、数字孪生等表征范式的跃迁是如何发生的。

Method: TBER提出了一个递归自举框架，核心是"解释性不充分"概念——表征并非因为"错误"而需要更换，而是因为其解释域被超越。形式化为五阶段过程：(1) 稳定观察（当前表征可解释观测）；(2) 异常检测（观测与表征预期不符）；(3) 解释性不充分识别（意识到表征无法使观测的组织和变换变得可理解）；(4) 表征涌现（新表征诞生）；(5) 暂态稳定（新表征暂时满足解释需求）。

Result: 该框架可应用于表征学习、潜在空间、基础模型、世界模型、数字孪生、自适应生物系统和科学发现等多个领域。指出未来的AI系统可能需要内置检测自身内部表征解释极限的机制。

Conclusion: 表征创新不仅是数据和算力的函数，更是解释性不充分驱动的递归过程。TBER为理解表征层次跃迁提供了系统性框架，并为构建能够自主识别表征局限并产生新表征的AI系统指明了方向。

Abstract: Representation learning is central to modern machine learning, enabling transitions from handcrafted features to learned embeddings, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, and digital twins. Yet most research examines how representations are optimized after a representational framework has been selected, while less attention is given to when a new level of representation becomes necessary. We introduce the Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence (TBER), a framework describing how new representations arise when existing ones become explanatorily insufficient. In this view, representational innovation is not only driven by more data, larger models, or greater computational power, but also by persistent explanatory gaps: situations in which a representation can still describe observations but can no longer make their organization or transformations intelligible. TBER identifies explanatory insufficiency as a positive signal for representational transition. A representation becomes insufficient not because it is necessarily false, but because its explanatory domain has been exceeded. The bootstrap dynamic follows a recursive sequence: observations reveal anomalies; anomalies expose insufficiencies; insufficiencies motivate new representations; and these new representations generate further observations and possible new insufficiencies.We formalize this process through five stages: stabilized observation, anomaly detection, recognition of explanatory insufficiency, representational emergence, and provisional stabilization. We discuss applications to representation learning, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, digital twins, adaptive biological systems, and scientific discovery. TBER suggests that future AI systems may benefit from mechanisms for detecting the explanatory limits of their own internal representations.

</details>


### [86] [TabSwift: An Efficient Tabular Foundation Model with Row-Wise Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07345)
*Si-Yang Liu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabSwift，通过轻量级行注意力机制、门控注意力稳定机制和可学习的寄存器token，在保持与TabPFN v2等强模型竞争性能的同时显著降低推理成本，并引入自适应早期退出机制实现动态推理深度调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（如TabPFN）虽性能优异，但为提升准确率不断引入复杂架构导致推理成本高、实际部署受限，因此需要在保持竞争力的同时提高推理效率。

Method: 采用轻量级逐行注意力骨干网络，添加门控注意力稳定机制和少量可学习寄存器token提供全局上下文并改善预训练质量；针对延迟敏感场景，引入逐层自适应早期退出机制，根据样本复杂度动态调整推理深度。

Result: TabSwift在分类和回归任务上与TabPFN v2、TabICL等更强的基础模型竞争力相当，同时推理效率更高。

Conclusion: TabSwift通过精简设计实现了高效且支持随时推理的表格上下文学习，适合实际部署场景。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, exemplified by TabPFN, perform prediction via in-context learning, inferring test labels directly from labeled training examples. They have demonstrated competitive performance, particularly on small-to-medium datasets. However, recent tabular foundation models often improve accuracy with increasingly complex architectures, incurring higher inference cost and limiting practical deployment. In this work, we revisit the original TabPFN design and show that a lightweight row-wise attention-only backbone can remain highly competitive with two simple enhancements: a gated attention stabilization mechanism and a small set of learnable register tokens that provide global context and improve pretraining quality. The resulting model, TabSwift, supports both classification and regression, and is competitive with stronger tabular foundation models (e.g., TabPFN v2 and TabICL) while being more efficient at inference. For latency-sensitive serving, we further introduce an adaptive layer-wise early-exit mechanism that dynamically adjusts inference depth per sample. Overall, TabSwift enables efficient and anytime tabular in-context learning for practical deployments.

</details>


### [87] [Breaking the Ice: Analyzing Cold Start Latency in vLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07362)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Animesh Trivedi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地分析了vLLM推理引擎的启动延迟，将其分解为六个基础步骤，发现其受CPU限制，并建立了轻量级分析模型用于预测和资源规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得日益重要。尽管vLLM已成为许多推理工作负载的事实标准引擎，但由于其复杂性和快速演进，尚缺乏对其启动延迟的系统性研究。

Method: 将vLLM启动过程分解为六个基础步骤，进行详细的性能表征；分析每个步骤相对于模型级和系统级参数的一致可解释缩放趋势；基于这些洞察构建轻量级分析模型。

Result: 证明vLLM启动过程主要受CPU限制，每个步骤均展现出一致且可解释的缩放趋势；所构建的轻量级分析模型能准确预测给定硬件配置下的启动延迟。

Conclusion: 该研究为大规模推理环境中的资源规划提供了可操作的指导，所有基准数据集、分析工具和预测脚本均已开源。

Abstract: As scalable inference services become popular, the cold start latency of an inference engine becomes important. Today, vLLM has evolved into the de facto inference engine of choice for many inference workloads. Although popular, due to its complexity and rapid evolution, there has not been a systematic study of its startup latency. With major architectural innovations such as the V1 API and the introduction of torch.compile, this paper presents the first detailed performance characterization of vLLM startup latency. We break down the startup process into six foundational steps and demonstrate that it is predominantly CPU bound. Each step exhibits consistent and interpretable scaling trends with respect to model-level and system-level parameters, enabling fine-grained attribution of latency sources. Building on these insights, we develop a lightweight analytical model that accurately predicts vLLM startup latency for a given hardware configuration, providing actionable guidance for resource planning in large-scale inference environments. All benchmarking datasets, analysis tools, and prediction scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler.

</details>


### [88] [Self-evolving LLM agents with in-distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07367)
*Yudi Zhang,Meng Fang,Zhenfang Chen,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Evolve提出了一种自演化框架，通过在分布内强化学习范式中统一过程奖励自动标注与策略学习，解决了LLM智能体在稀疏奖励长程决策中的信用分配问题，在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂环境中进行可靠的长程决策面临根本性挑战，核心困难在于信用分配（credit assignment）问题——智能体通常仅在回合结束时获得延迟奖励，缺乏中间过程的密集监督信号。

Method: 1) 在每轮演化迭代中，从混合离策略数据集（包含专家演示与智能体自生成轨迹）学习分布内评论家（in-distribution critic），通过加权隐式Q学习（Weighted Implicit Q-Learning）目标稳定稀疏奖励下的Bellman备份；2) 利用学到的价值函数通过优势估计推导逐步骤过程奖励（process rewards），无需环境回溯或人工标注；3) 使用这些信号进行行为邻近策略优化（behavior-proximal policy optimization），在用于过程奖励标注的数据上迭代演化智能体，避免加剧分布偏移。

Result: 在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上，Q-Evolve在样本效率、鲁棒性和整体任务性能方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将过程级监督与策略在共享的分布内学习循环中共同演化，可以实现稳定的智能体自演化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful controllers for interactive agents in complex environments, yet training them to perform reliable long-horizon decision making remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in credit assignment: agents often receive delayed rewards only at the end of episodes. In this paper, we propose Q-Evolve, a self-evolving framework for LLM agents that unifies automatic process-reward labeling and policy learning within a principled in-distribution reinforcement learning paradigm. In each evolving iteration, our method learns an in-distribution critic from a hybrid off-policy dataset that combines expert demonstrations with agent-generated trajectories, stabilizing Bellman backups in sparse-reward settings via a weighted Implicit Q-Learning objective. The learned value function is then used to derive step-wise process rewards through advantage estimation, enabling dense and reliable supervision without environment backtracking or human annotation. Leveraging these signals, we perform behavior-proximal policy optimization that evolves the agent over the data used for process reward labeling, allowing iterative self-improvement without exacerbating distribution shift. We evaluate our method on AlfWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, showing Q-Evolve outperforms strong baselines in sample efficiency, robustness, and overall task performance. Our results demonstrate that stable agent self-evolution is achievable through the co-evolution of process-level supervision and policy, both grounded within a shared in-distribution learning loop.

</details>


### [89] [Making the Most of Limited Data: Score-Aware Training for Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07387)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Tzu-Hung Huang,Chih-Pin Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出score-aware训练方法，通过CLAP评分信号指导训练过程，包括Beta噪声调度、段级过滤、两阶段描述和REPA辅助损失，在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge中获得排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到音乐生成系统依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级计算资源，无法区分架构贡献和资源优势，需要一种更高效、数据利用率更高的方法。

Method: 1) score-aware training: 使用CLAP评分作为直接监督信号；2) CLAP条件Beta噪声时间表：将低分片段导向高噪声训练区域，作为隐式正则化；3) 段级过滤：移除最不匹配的样本；4) 两阶段描述流程：弥合训练描述与推理提示之间的分布差距；5) REPA辅助损失：从预训练CLAP和MuQ编码器转移结构化语义知识。

Result: 450M参数的FluxAudio系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge客观评估中两个赛道均排名第二，在效率赛道最终MOS评估中排名第三。

Conclusion: score-aware训练方法能在不依赖大规模数据和计算资源的情况下有效提升文本到音乐生成质量，验证了利用对齐评分信号指导训练过程的可行性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.

</details>


### [90] [Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07400)
*Stefan Ivanovic,Ge Liu,Mohammed El-Kebir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GReinSS框架，通过动态缩放奖励的策略学习来推断组合隐状态，在模拟数据和真实RNA测序数据中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题需要从间接观测中推断未观测到的机制性隐状态，但经典方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合爆炸的大空间，而深度学习方法（如VAE）通常学习人工隐状态而非真实的机制性隐状态。

Method: GReinSS采用策略学习框架，利用动态缩放的奖励（dynamically rescaled rewards）来学习最大化观测数据似然的隐状态分布。

Result: GReinSS在模拟的隐集合和隐图重建任务上优于替代策略学习和生成建模基线；在真实短读长RNA-seq数据上重建的异构体比标准RSEM算法更好地匹配长读长测序检测到的异构体。

Conclusion: GReinSS是一种从间接观测中对组合隐状态进行生成式建模和推断的既有理论原则又有实际效果的方法。

Abstract: Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.

</details>


### [91] [Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07404)
*Rohan Shravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在单个8-GPU节点上端到端训练千亿参数稀疏混合专家模型LightningLM 0.1V的方法。模型从1.78B稠密种子通过四个阶段增长至120B（460个路由专家，top-12路由），活跃参数仅5.93B。核心技术包括：可逆循环栈保持激活内存平坦、状态保持增长策略、以及TQP量化策略（将优化器状态从100B+降至2.26B适配器参数）。模型族、分词器和训练代码均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在资源受限的单节点（仅8个GPU）上训练大规模稀疏MoE语言模型的可行性，通过系统性增长策略和工程优化突破硬件限制，同时保持模型能力。

Method: 采用四阶段增长策略：稠密种子→5B MoE→9B MoE→120B MoE（460专家，top-12路由）。核心技术包括：(1) 可逆循环栈，反向传播时重建激活值而非存储，保持激活内存平坦；(2) 状态保持增长，各扩展阶段（稠密到MoE、浅到深、少专家到多专家）均有可复现原则；(3) TQP量化策略，对专家基础权重量化，仅训练2.26B低秩适配器参数，使专家路径优化器状态减少约45倍。

Result: 成功在单节点8 GPU上训练120B参数MoE模型（活跃参数5.93B），8K上下文，达到1.78的训练损失。模型在特定领域（多语言印地语能力、代码）的留出损失证明目标能力通过构造学习。

Conclusion: 本研究的新颖之处不在于单一原语创新，而在于将已知原语集成整合：在单节点上端到端运行完整的增长谱系，以实践者级别记录，并提供按领域划分的留出损失作为能力证据。模型族、分词器和训练代码均已开源发布。

Abstract: This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale.
  This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45.
  What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.

</details>


### [92] [Sparsely gated tiny linear experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07414)
*Simon Schug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种极稀疏门控线性神经元网络（sgatlin），将每个专家缩减为单个神经元并移除非线性激活，在同等计算量下提升语言模型困惑度，同时增强了可解释性，无需额外训练即可解释前馈电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合专家模型虽然稀疏，但单个专家仍然是大型密集层，计算效率与可解释性仍有提升空间。作者希望进一步增加稀疏性，同时探索线性化对计算效率和可解释性的影响。

Method: 将transformer前馈层替换为sgatlin：每个专家仅含单个神经元，采用稀疏门控机制从大量神经元中选取极小一部分激活，并移除专家内部的非线性激活函数（如ReLU/GELU），形成纯线性门控网络。

Result: 在等计算量（isoflop）对比下，sgatlin在多个计算预算下均优于传统transformer前馈层。在可解释性案例研究中，sgatlin的前馈电路形成语义结构化的聚类，并对事实回忆具有因果关联。

Conclusion: 极稀疏线性门控神经元网络为构建计算高效且可解释的transformer前馈层提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Sparsity allows scaling model parameters without proportionally increasing computational cost. While mixture of experts (MoE) models are made increasingly sparse, individual experts typically remain large and dense. Here, we demonstrate that further increasing sparsity by shrinking each expert to consist of a single neuron and selecting a tiny fraction of many available neurons can improve compute efficiency and interpretability. Counterintuitively, the key to achieving both is removing the nonlinearity typically applied to the experts, resulting in a network of sparsely gated linear neurons (sgatlin). In an isoflop comparison, we find that replacing all transformer feedforward layers with sgatlin improves perplexity in language models across different compute budgets. At the same time, the sparsity and linearity of the resulting feedforward circuits present new opportunities for model interpretability. In a small-scale case study, we demonstrate that feedforward circuits in sgatlin can be interpreted without having to train additional replacement models. We find that they form semantically structured clusters and are causally implicated in factual recall. Our findings paint a possible path towards compute-efficient and interpretable transformer feedforward layers.

</details>


### [93] [Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07416)
*Abhijeet Praveen,Sareh Soleimani,Cormac Cureton,Aman Sidhu,Kintak Raymond Yu,Cristian Cojocaru,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用高速视频观测等离子体羽流来预测大气等离子喷涂（APS）过程中飞行粒子温度与速度的方法。通过三种视频特征表示和多种机器学习模型（TabPFN、CNN、随机森林等）进行实验，发现基于原始视频帧的预训练CNN模型表现最佳，温度和速度预测R²分别达到0.90和0.82，表明视频衍生的羽流信息可用于非侵入式APS诊断和实时过程监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APS过程中飞行粒子的温度和速度强烈影响涂层质量，但这些粒子特性难以在操作过程中连续监测，因此需要开发非侵入式的数据驱动诊断方法。

Method: 提出三种视频衍生的特征表示，使用TabPFN、CNN、随机森林、梯度提升、SVR和XGBoost等模型进行预测。采用分组留一交叉验证，对63次APS喷涂实验的126个带标签的喷涂前后视频记录进行评估。同时评估了直接基于原始视频帧的预训练CNN模型。

Result: TabPFN在工程化特征实验中温度预测最稳定（R²=0.86），CNN在速度预测上表现更佳（R²=0.81）。基于原始视频帧的预训练CNN模型性能最高，温度和速度预测R²分别达到0.90和0.82。

Conclusion: 视频衍生的羽流信息为非侵入式APS诊断和实时过程监控提供了有前景且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

</details>


### [94] [Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07426)
*Hanqiao Yu,Shusen Yang,Xuebin Ren,Cong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Deflex的端到端AI方法，能够自动从多尺度复杂系统中提取不同形式的数学公式（包括不变量和分布），在六个代表性系统中比现有方法效率提升最多7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI方法在单尺度系统中表现良好，但难以识别多尺度复杂系统中具有不同形式的尺度特定公式，需要一种能自动发现多尺度公式的方法。

Method: Deflex由两个子系统组成：Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表示）和Deflexpressor（基于lambda演算的符号回归模型，用于高阶公式）。Deflexpressor生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，后者通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来指导公式发现。

Result: 在六个具有不同行为特征的典型复杂系统上，Deflex相比现有最优方法实现了最高7倍的效率提升，并实现了自动化的多尺度公式发现。

Conclusion: Deflex可作为跨学科科学发现的有用工具，为从复杂系统中自动提取多尺度数学公式提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas. Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.

</details>


### [95] [Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07474)
*Mohammadreza Sadeghi,Sareh Soleimani,Zihan Wang,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督持续聚类（UCC）新问题，并引入前向-后向知识蒸馏框架（FBCC），通过持续教师网络与轻量任务特定学生，在不存储历史数据的情况下实现聚类结构的持续学习与遗忘抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督持续学习（UCL）方法主要关注分类任务，忽略了聚类特定目标；同时，已有的缓解灾难性遗忘策略（如知识蒸馏、重放缓冲）存在内存与隐私问题。本文旨在填补UCL中聚类目标被忽视的空白。

Method: 提出FBCC框架，包含一个持续教师网络（带聚类投影头）和多个轻量级任务特定学生网络。通过双阶段前向-后向蒸馏过程：前向阶段将教师知识蒸馏给学生，后向阶段将学生学到的聚类信息反馈给教师，使教师在不存储历史数据的情况下学习新聚类同时保留旧聚类结构。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，并显著降低了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: FBCC是UCC领域的开创性方法，通过双向知识蒸馏有效解决了无监督持续聚类中的灾难性遗忘问题，无需存储历史数据，在隐私和内存方面具有优势。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [96] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将新型生成式漂移框架（generative drifting）应用于流体力学，通过条件架构在VAE潜空间执行漂移并使用标签感知掩码对齐边界条件，实现了与迭代扩散相当的精度但速度快两个数量级，为实时CFD替代模型提供了高效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD虽能提供高保真流场用于优化室内环境，但其计算成本限制了快速探索；现有生成式替代模型虽比确定性网络更好地建模分布，但迭代采样速度慢。

Method: 将生成式漂移框架适配到流体力学，设计条件架构在学习的VAE潜空间中执行漂移，并采用标签感知掩码（label-aware masking）使生成样本与边界条件对齐；同时提出空间条件变体以泛化到未见几何结构。

Result: 标签条件模型在精度和流一致性上匹配迭代扩散方法，运行速度快两个数量级；空间条件变体展现出对未见几何结构的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: 条件漂移可作为扩散方法的高效替代方案，在推理速度关键的应用场景中实现实时CFD替代模型。

Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

</details>


### [97] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种持续元学习框架，通过贝叶斯高斯混合模型实现个性化心脏神经代理的持续学习，避免灾难性遗忘，并能识别新数据来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于元学习的个性化心脏模拟方法假设训练分布静态且已知任务标识，当新数据流式到达时需要完全重训练以避免灾难性遗忘，这在临床场景中不可行。

Method: 提出持续元学习框架，利用持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型（Bayesian GMM）管理内存缓冲区，动态推断数据标识符及其关系，实现持续整合信息并识别数据来源是否已知。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上，该方法在仿真预测、计算可扩展性及抗灾难性遗忘方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该持续元学习框架能够在不完全重训练的情况下，持续适应新数据并保持旧任务性能，适用于数据流式到达的临床场景。

Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [98] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经网络的二阶路径核插值公式，在Domingos一阶公式基础上，引入曲率加权项和随机梯度噪声耦合项，并扩展到带动量的SGD，同时给出了预测浓度的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练数据如何塑造神经网络预测是现代学习理论的核心问题。Domingos (2020) 提出的一阶插值公式仅适用于确定性梯度下降，尚缺乏对曲率信息（二阶效应）以及随机优化（SGD、动量SGD）情况下更精细的刻画。

Method: 基于路径核框架，将插值公式展开到二阶，推导出曲率加权的插值项；针对SGD引入梯度噪声协方差与预测曲率的耦合项；进一步将表示扩展到带动量的SGD，并建立预测浓度的集中估计。

Result: 得到了二阶插值公式：主项仍为一阶路径核插值，补充了曲率加权项；SGD下出现噪声-曲率耦合项；动量SGD保持插值结构但权重被记忆因子修改；给出了预测围绕二阶期望表示的波动尺度浓度界。

Conclusion: 这些结果为神经网络预测的路径核解释提供了精细化的二阶修正，统一了确定性GD和随机SGD（含动量）下的插值表示框架。

Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [A Primal--Dual Penalty Algorithm and Optimal Control of the Double Integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06756)
*Regina S. Burachik,C. Yalçın Kaya,Xuemei Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了 primal-dual 惩罚方案在双积分器最优控制问题所有边界条件下的行为，推导了对偶函数和最优控制变量的闭式表达式，给出了最小精确惩罚参数的完整表征，分析了约束函数的迭代轨迹，并比较了两种步长规则与 Polyak 型步长策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解和分析 primal-dual 惩罚方案在双积分器最优控制问题所有可能实例（不同边界条件）下的行为特性，特别是惩罚参数、迭代动力学和步长规则的影响。

Method: 采用基于对偶的框架推导闭式解；构造并分析约束函数轨迹以研究迭代动力学；对比两种步长规则与 Polyak 型步长策略。

Result: 获得了对偶函数和最优控制变量的闭式表达式；完整刻画了最小精确惩罚参数；揭示了算法在不同边界条件下的迭代动力学特性。

Conclusion: 该对偶框架有效解决了双积分器最优控制问题，所提出的步长规则与 Polyak 型策略相比具有竞争力。

Abstract: We investigate the behaviour of a primal--dual penalty scheme applied to all possible instances, i.e., boundary conditions, of an optimal control problem involving the double integrator. The proposed duality-based framework enables us to derive closed-form expressions for the associated dual functions as well as for the optimal control variables. Furthermore, we provide a complete characterization of the least exact penalty parameter for this problem. The iteration dynamics of the algorithm are examined by constructing and analyzing the trajectories of the constraint functions for every admissible instance of the problem. Finally, we compare the two step-size rules employed in our primal--dual algorithm with two alternative Polyak-type step-size strategies.

</details>


### [100] [Successive Convexification for Trajectory Optimization with Continuous-time Satisfaction of Signal Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06896)
*Samet Uzun,Behçet Açıkmeşe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逐次凸化框架的轨迹优化方法，用于处理连续时间信号时序逻辑（CT-STL）规范。该框架利用基于广义均值的鲁棒性（GMSR）作为可微分的CT-STL约束构建块，结合时间伸缩、有限维控制参数化、多重打点离散化以及具有收敛保证的逐次凸规划方法（prox-convex）来求解非凸问题。主要实现通过将时间聚合嵌入到增广的连续时间动力学中，实现与转录无关的平滑CT-STL参数化。还讨论了一种互补的稠密时间实现方法。实验在双积分器系统和六自由度四旋翼飞行器上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹优化方法在处理连续时间STL规范时面临挑战：标准定量语义存在局部性和梯度掩蔽行为，不利于基于梯度的优化；缺乏一种可微、平滑且精确的CT-STL约束参数化方法，难以集成到现有最优控制管线中。

Method: 1) 采用广义均值鲁棒性（GMSR）作为离散时间STL的光滑精确参数化；2) 将其扩展到连续时间域，通过时间聚合嵌入增广动力学实现CT-STL约束；3) 集成时间伸缩处理自由终端时间问题；4) 采用有限维控制参数化和多重打点离散化动力学；5) 使用具有收敛保证的prox-convex方法求解非凸优化问题；6) 提出互补的稠密时间实现方法。

Result: 在双积分器系统上验证了包含always、eventually和until规范的成功轨迹优化；在六自由度四旋翼飞行器问题上验证了包含always、implication和eventually组合规范的可行性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 提出的GMSR-based CT-STL框架有效缓解了标准语义的局部性和梯度掩蔽问题，提供了有利于梯度优化的景观；框架具有转录无关性，易于集成到现有最优控制管线中，并通过正则化参数控制精度。

Abstract: This paper presents a successive convexification framework for trajectory optimization under continuous-time Signal Temporal Logic (CT-STL) specifications. The framework employs generalized mean-based robustness (GMSR), a smooth and exact parameterization of discrete-time STL, as a logical building block for constructing differentiable CT-STL constraints in optimal control. It is integrated with time-dilation for free-final-time problems, finite-dimensional control parameterization, multiple-shooting discretization of the dynamics, and a convergence-guaranteed sequential convex programming method, prox-convex, to solve the nonconvex program. The main CT-STL realization embeds temporal aggregation into augmented continuous-time dynamics. This augmentation-based construction is largely transcription-independent, can be incorporated into existing optimal-control pipelines with minimal structural changes, and enables smooth CT-STL parameterizations with accuracy controlled by a user-selected regularization parameter. We also discuss a complementary dense-time realization that evaluates CT-STL formulas directly on the integration subnodes used for dynamics discretization, yielding a smooth and exact parameterization on the numerical trajectory representation, up to the accuracy of the integration scheme. The proposed GMSR-based formulations mitigate the locality and gradient-masking behavior of standard quantitative semantics and therefore provide a favorable landscape for gradient-based trajectory optimization. The framework is demonstrated through trajectory-optimization examples for a double-integrator system with continuous-time \always{}, \eventually{}, and \until{} specifications, and a 6-DoF quadrotor flight problem with combined \always{}, \implication{}, and \eventually{}-type specifications. The implementation is available at https://github.com/UW-ACL/TrajOpt_CT-STL.

</details>


### [101] [Scalable Finite Adaptability via Polyhedral Partition and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06927)
*Zolykha Rezaei,Ningji Wei,Eojin Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于显式多面体划分的有限适应性决策框架，通过参数化划分策略和近似-学习-并行框架，在保证策略收敛性的同时显著提升大规模不确定优化问题的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限适应性方法在处理不确定性决策时面临两大可扩展性挑战：(i) 将决策分配给不确定性实现具有组合特性；(ii) 不确定性集划分与后续决策需要联合优化。亟需一种既能保证最优性又能提升计算效率的方法。

Method: (1) 提出显式多面体划分框架，使不确定性集划分透明化；(2) 证明在温和正则条件下，随区域数增加策略收敛到完全自适应策略；(3) 构建参数化划分框架，支持鲁棒和随机有限适应性问题的易处理重构；(4) 提出近似-学习-并行(ALP)框架，集成划分学习与并行优化。

Result: 在经典鲁棒和随机测试集上的计算实验表明，所提方法能够扩展到更大规模实例，并取得有竞争力的策略性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过显式多面体划分和参数化设计，有效解决了有限适应性决策的可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模不确定性决策提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: We study finite adaptability for decision-making under uncertainty, where a small set of candidate solutions is prepared in advance and the best response is selected after uncertainty is realized. While existing methods have made significant progress on exact formulations, scalability remains a persistent challenge due to (i) the combinatorial nature of assigning decisions to uncertainty realizations, and (ii) the joint optimization of uncertainty set partition and subsequent decisions. We propose a framework that makes the partition of the uncertainty set explicit and uses polyhedral partitions as the basis for policy design. Under mild regularity conditions and for general risk measures, we show that such policies converge to the optimal fully adjustable policy as the number of regions increases. Building on this result, we develop a parametric partition framework that allows flexible policy design with tractable reformulations for both robust and stochastic finite adaptability problems. To improve scalability, we introduce an approximate-learn-parallel framework that integrates partition learning with parallel optimization while preserving solution robustness. Computational experiments on classical testbeds in both robust and stochastic settings show that the proposed method scales to larger instances and yields competitive policy performance.

</details>


### [102] [A Single-Loop Regularized Newton Method for Nonconvex-Strongly-Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07003)
*Bohao Ma,Nachuan Xiao,Junyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对非凸-强凹极小极大问题，提出一种基于正则化极小化重构的单循环二阶方法框架，通过自适应三次-四次上界技术，在确定性和随机设定下均取得优于双循环方法的理论复杂度和实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶方法采用双循环结构，内循环对最大化子问题求解到高精度，常用一阶方法虽可扩展但破坏了二阶方法的条件不敏感性，限制了其在病态问题上的应用。作者希望设计一种单循环框架来消除内循环瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将原始极小极大问题等价转化为正则化极小化问题；2) 推导自适应三次-四次上界（adaptive cubic-quadratic majorization）来动态吸收重构Hessian的非Lipschitz分量；3) 在随机设定中结合递归方差缩减技术。

Result: 确定性设定：全局迭代复杂度 O(ε^{-1.5}) 匹配双循环二阶方法，同时实现局部超线性收敛（现有双循环方法因内循环瓶颈无法达到）；随机设定：梯度复杂度 O(ε^{-3})、Hessian复杂度 O(ε^{-2})，均比双循环方法提升 O(ε^{-0.5}) 因子；实验验证显著优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 提出的单循环框架有效克服了双循环二阶方法的内循环瓶颈，在理论和实践上均取得更优性能。作为副产品，还修复了非凸极小化随机二阶方法中的一个已知技术缺陷。

Abstract: For smooth nonconvex-strongly-concave minimax problems, existing second-order methods share a common double-loop structure where the inner maximization is solved to sufficiently high accuracy before each second-order step. First-order methods are often adopted in the inner loops for scalability, but they also undermine the condition-insensitivity of second-order methods, limiting these methods to instances with mild conditioning. To resolve this issue, we propose a novel single-loop framework based on an equivalent regularized minimization reformulation of the original problem. By deriving a new adaptive cubic-quadratic majorization to dynamically absorb the non-Lipschitz components of the reformulated Hessian, we establish a regularized Newton method with robust theoretical guarantees across multiple settings. For deterministic problems, our single-loop method matches the $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ global iteration complexity of the existing double-loop second-order methods, while automatically achieving a local superlinear rate that is unavailable in existing works due to the inner-loop bottleneck. For the stochastic setting, we achieve $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ gradient and $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ Hessian complexities by integrating a recursive variance reduction, strictly improving those of the double-loop methods by $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-0.5})$ factors. In both deterministic and stochastic experiments, our methods significantly outperform the benchmarks, offering substantial speedups over the double-loop methods even under mildly conditioned instances. As a byproduct of our analysis, we close a gap in stochastic second-order methods for nonconvex minimization, where the best known result contains a nontrivial technical issue.

</details>


### [103] [A Min-Max Gradient Search Method for Constrained Simulation Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07029)
*Ruiyang Jin,Siyang Gao,Henry Lam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为min-max gradient search (MGS)的单循环算法，用于求解受约束的仿真优化问题。MGS采用原始-对偶框架与随机梯度估计器，交替进行梯度下降和上升，首次为单循环CSO算法建立了有限时间收敛保证，收敛率为$\tilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$，并在排队系统和2000维优化问题上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束仿真优化(CSO)在实际中广泛应用，但仿真约束缺乏可解析计算的梯度，使得开发高效求解方法和建立非渐近理论保证比无约束情形更具挑战性。现有方法多基于随机逼近，尚无单循环算法的有限时间收敛性分析。

Method: 提出MGS算法，将原始-对偶框架与随机梯度估计器相结合，对原始变量进行梯度下降、对偶变量进行梯度上升的交替更新，以在优化目标的同时惩罚约束违反。

Result: 首次建立了单循环CSO算法的有限时间收敛保证：MGS以$\tilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$的速率收敛到平稳解（在温和条件下为KKT点）。在序列排队系统和2000维优化问题上的数值实验验证了方法的优越性能和可扩展性。

Conclusion: MGS算法有效填补了约束仿真优化中单循环方法在非渐近理论分析方面的空白，其收敛率保证和实验表现表明该方法兼具理论严谨性与实际可扩展性。

Abstract: Constrained simulation optimization (CSO) is a general framework for optimizing stochastic systems under performance constraints. It arises widely in practice where objective and constraint evaluations are available only through noisy simulation outputs. Compared with the unconstrained setting, the lack of accessible analytical gradients for simulation-based constraints makes it more challenging to develop efficient solution methods and establish non-asymptotic guarantees. To address this gap, we propose a novel single-loop algorithm, called min-max gradient search (MGS), which integrates a primal-dual framework with stochastic gradient estimators. Unlike conventional stochastic approximation methods based on gradient descent for solving simulation optimization problems, such as Zhou and Bhatnagar (2017) and Hu and Fu (2025), MGS performs alternating gradient descent and ascent on the primal and dual variables, which improves the objective while penalizing constraint violations. For the first time, we establish a finite-time convergence guarantee for single-loop CSO algorithms by showing that MGS converges to a stationary solution (a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker point under mild conditions) at a rate of $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$, where $T$ is the number of iterations. Numerical experiments on a serial queuing system and a 2000-dimensional optimization problem demonstrate the superior performance and scalability of MGS.

</details>


### [104] [Explicit Convergence Regions of PID-Damped Accelerated Gradient Methods in Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07340)
*Ailun Jian,Xun Li,Weigang Sun,Gaohang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过将PID阻尼加速梯度方法建模为三阶非线性反馈动力系统，利用KYP引理建立了步长、动量和导数增益的三维显式收敛区域，揭示了非凸曲率对导数增益的严格几何上限，为过阻尼停滞现象提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PID阻尼加速梯度方法能缓解经典动量方法的过冲和振荡问题，但步长、动量与导数增益之间的耦合使得显式收敛区域难以解析刻画，缺乏明确的理论收敛边界。

Method: 将该类算法建模为三阶非线性反馈动力系统，基于Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov (KYP)引理进行鲁棒控制理论分析，推导出步长、动量和导数增益的三维显式收敛区域。

Result: 建立了在Regularity Condition下保证线性收敛的显式三维收敛区域；揭示了非凸曲率对导数增益的严格几何上界，超出该上界会导致过阻尼严重收缩可行步长区域。

Conclusion: 数值实验验证了理论边界，并为导数增益提供了实用参数选择指南，从理论上解释了过阻尼停滞现象。

Abstract: Momentum-based accelerated gradient methods are widely adopted to expedite convergence in nonconvex optimization, but are prone to overshooting and oscillatory behavior. A class of PID-damped accelerated gradient methods mitigates this issue by augmenting classical momentum methods with a discrete-time derivative damping term. However, the coupling among the step size, momentum, and derivative gain renders the explicit characterization of their convergence regions analytically intractable, leaving explicit theoretical convergence boundaries unexplored. In this paper, we model this class of algorithms as a third-order nonlinear feedback dynamical system and establish explicit three-dimensional convergence regions for the step size, momentum, and derivative gain via a robust control-theoretic analysis based on the Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov (KYP) lemma, formally guaranteeing linear convergence under the Regularity Condition. Furthermore, we reveal a strict geometric upper bound on the derivative gain dictated by the nonconvex curvature, beyond which over-damping severely contracts the feasible step-size region, providing a rigorous theoretical explanation for the overdamped stagnation phenomenon. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical boundaries and illustrate practical parameter selection guidelines for the derivative gain.

</details>


### [105] [The Proxy Benders Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07403)
*Changkun Guan,El Mehdi Er Raqabi,Mathieu Tanneau,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出代理Benders分解（Proxy-BD），在Benders分解框架中用经过认证的优化代理替代重复精确求解子问题，通过自监督预测-投影-补全机制产生对偶可行解以生成有效Benders割。在大规模设施选址和网络设计问题上，该方法在保持接近最优解质量的同时，实现了高达161倍的加速比，并将生成割的数量减少240倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Benders分解在迭代过程中反复求解高度相似的子问题，且存在锯齿行为，导致大规模场景下收敛缓慢。本文旨在利用子问题的重复结构和参数化特性，用代理模型替代重复精确求解，提升计算效率。

Method: 提出Proxy-BD框架，核心是自监督的预测-投影-补全机制：首先预测对偶变量，然后通过投影和补全认证层确保对偶可行性，从而生成理论上有效的Benders割。该框架保持分解的理论有效性，且可自然扩展到branch-and-Benders-cut等现代分解方案。

Result: 在2000x2000规模的设施选址问题上，中位最优性间隙低于0.5%，中位加速比高达161倍，生成割数量减少240倍以上。计算收益随问题复杂度增加而增加，表明代理推理在大规模场景下优于重复精确求解。

Conclusion: Proxy-BD通过代理优化替代精确求解，显著降低了子问题计算开销，同时保持解质量和理论有效性，在大规模分解优化中展现出优越的扩展性。

Abstract: Benders decomposition is a fundamental framework for solving large-scale mixed-integer optimization problems with complicating variables that, when fixed, yield significantly easier subproblems. However, classical Benders decomposition repeatedly solves highly similar subproblems and often exhibits zigzagging behavior across iterations, leading to slow convergence in large-scale settings. Motivated by the repetitive structure and parametric nature of Benders subproblems, this paper introduces the proxy Benders decomposition (Proxy-BD), a new decomposition framework in which subproblem optimization is replaced by certified optimization proxies rather than repeated exact solves.
  The proposed proxy follows a self-supervised predict-project-and-complete mechanism that produces dual-feasible solutions for generating provably valid Benders cuts. The framework preserves the theoretical validity of the decomposition independently of prediction quality through a projection-and-completion certification layer. A formal characterization of proxy-induced cuts is established, and the framework naturally extends to modern decomposition schemes, including branch-and-Benders-cut algorithms.
  Computational experiments on large-scale facility location and network design problems demonstrate that Proxy-BD substantially reduces the computational effort of subproblems while maintaining near-optimal solution quality. On large-scale uncapacitated facility location instances up to 2000x2000, Proxy-BD achieves median optimality gaps below 0.5%, yields up to 161x median speedups, and reduces the number of generated cuts by more than 240x on the largest instances. The computational gains consistently increase with recourse complexity, indicating that proxy-based inference scales substantially more favorably than repeated exact subproblem optimization in large-scale decomposition settings.

</details>


### [106] [A Nine-Compartment Nonlinear Epidemic Model with Spline-Based Identification of Time-Varying Transmission and Vaccination Dynamics: Application to the COVID-19 Third Wave in Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07413)
*Lokman Rachid Melhani,Antonino Sferlazza,Lars Grüne,Dominique Persano Adorno,Filippo D'Ippolito,Omar Enzo Santangelo,Ivan Marchese,Antonino Lo Burgio,Alberto Firenze*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含两种共循环病毒株、超级传播者亚群、部分疫苗免疫（含衰减）、以及明确住院动态和差异化死亡率的九仓室非线性流行病模型。利用意大利2021年1-5月COVID-19数据，通过PCHIP控制节点参数化将校准简化为14变量SQP问题，实现了对住院人数（R²=0.966）、累计死亡（R²=0.987）和累计疫苗接种（R²=0.999）的高精度拟合，并建立了模型的适定性、基本再生数、稳定性、可辨识性及误差界等理论分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多病毒株共循环、超级传播、疫苗接种及免疫衰减等复杂因素下的流行病建模需求，现有模型难以同时纳入这些机制并进行有效参数辨识，亟需一个既能精确拟合多源数据又具备严格理论保证的数学框架。

Method: 采用九仓室非线性ODE模型，将病毒传播率和疫苗接种率建模为时变控制输入，通过PCHIP参数化将无限维参数估计转化为14个控制节点的SQP问题，结合参数自举（n=1000）量化不确定性；理论分析包括基本再生数闭式解、平衡点稳定性、PCHIP逼近误差界（O(h²)）、Fisher信息矩阵可辨识性与噪声稳定界、以及有效再生数持续低于1时的衰减充分条件。

Result: 模型对意大利数据拟合优度极高：住院人数R²=0.966、累计死亡R²=0.987、累计疫苗接种R²=0.999。敏感性分析显示医院吞吐量参数的影响超过传播率，表明反应性防控措施无法阻止由潜伏病毒载量驱动的住院高峰。

Conclusion: 本文在理论完备性（稳定性、可辨识性、收敛性）与实证精度之间取得了良好平衡，为多病毒株复杂流行病建模提供了可推广的范本。关键政策启示：防控资源应优先配置于医院吞吐能力而非仅降低传播率。

Abstract: We develop a nine-compartment nonlinear epidemic model incorporating two co-circulating viral strains (ancestral I1 and the Alpha variant B.1.1.7 I2, which is 43-90% more transmissible, c2=1.5), a super-spreader subpopulation, partial vaccine-induced immunity with waning, and explicit hospitalization dynamics with differentiated mortality. Transmission and vaccination rates are treated as time-varying control inputs and identified from Italian COVID-19 data (January-May 2021) via a Piecewise Cubic Hermite Interpolating Polynomial (PCHIP) control-node parameterization, reducing calibration to a fourteen-variable Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) problem with monotonicity and box constraints. A parametric bootstrap (n=1000) quantifies parameter uncertainty. The calibrated model achieves R^2=0.966 for active hospitalizations, R^2=0.987 for cumulative fatalities, and R^2=0.999 for cumulative vaccinations. Well-posedness, the basic reproduction number in closed form, and local and global stability of the disease-free equilibrium are established analytically. An L-infinity approximation error bound shows that the PCHIP control-node parameterization converges to the true time-varying parameters at rate O(h^2) as the node spacing vanishes. Local identifiability and a noise stability bound are established via the Fisher information matrix. A sufficient threshold condition proves epidemic decay under time-varying suppression whenever the effective reproduction number remains persistently below one. Sensitivity analyses consistently rank hospital throughput parameters above the transmission rate, providing a mathematical basis for the observation that reactive containment measures cannot prevent a hospitalization peak already driven by the pre-existing latent viral load.

</details>


### [107] [Local optimization of weak distance between compact surfaces on special Euclidean group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07440)
*Kazuki Koga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在特殊欧氏群上通过负阶Sobolev范数量化两个紧致曲面之间弱距离的局部优化方法，并利用非均匀快速傅里叶变换实现高效梯度计算，数值实验展示了SR1信赖域方法在求解问题中的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对嵌入三维欧氏空间中的两个紧致曲面，希望在特殊欧氏群上通过局部优化找到一个最优等距变换（旋转+平移），使得两个曲面在某种弱距离意义下尽可能对齐。

Method: 将曲面与其表面测度关联，利用Plancherel定理通过负阶Sobolev范数（非齐次）量化曲面间差异；施加等距变换相当于对表面测度做前推，从而将距离定义为Lie群上的函数；选取合适的Sobolev范数指数使距离函数的平方具有足够可微性，从而可进行基于梯度的局部优化；利用梯度函数的结构性特点，借助非均匀快速傅里叶变换（NUFFT）高效实现。

Result: 数值实验中，SR1信赖域方法在若干求根问题上观察到收敛性，并讨论了该方法与更几何量之间的联系。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于负阶Sobolev范数的弱距离在特殊欧氏群上具有良好的可微性，结合NUFFT可高效计算梯度，为曲面配准提供了一种新的局部优化框架。

Abstract: We consider local optimization of a weak distance between two compact surfaces embedded in the three-dimensional Euclidean space on its special Euclidean group. Identifying those objects with the associated surface measures, their discrepancy is quantified in terms of the inhomogeneous Sobolev norm of negative order via the Plancherel theorem. Then, applying an isometry to one surface corresponds to pushforwarding its surface measure and the distance can be regarded as a function on the Lie group. For appropriate exponents of the Sobolev norm, the second power of the function acquires sufficient differentiability that allows to search for its local minima in a derivative-based framework, and the gradient of the objective function has a favorable structure for efficient implementations using the nonuniform fast Fourier transform. In numerical experiments, we observe convergence of the SR1 trust-region method applied to a few root-finding problems and discuss its connection to a more geometric quantity.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Detecting and Mitigating Bias by Treating Fairness as a Symmetry Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06514)
*Nishit Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将公平性视为对称性破缺问题，通过反事实敏感属性翻转下的输出不变性定义公平分类器，并采用基于损失的规则化作为对称性恢复机制。在四个合成数据集上实现90%以上的偏差减少，准确率损失约5%。该方法无需因果图知识、计算轻量，适用于任何可定义为比特翻转的敏感属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的机器学习系统在社会经济场景中持续展现偏差，现有公平性方法通常需要因果图或复杂建模，缺乏通用且轻量的解决方案。

Method: 将偏差形式化为对称性破缺操作，通过反事实操作切换敏感属性（保持能力特征不变）定义分类器公平性，采用基于损失的规则化作为对称性恢复机制。

Result: 在四个合成数据集上，该框架实现了90%以上的违反率降低，准确率成本约5%。

Conclusion: 该框架无需因果图知识、计算轻量、适用于任何可定义为比特翻转的敏感属性，适合主流基准中缺失的本地歧视来源场景。

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in high stakes socioeconomic settings routinely display bias. We formalize bias as a symmetry breaking operation: a classifier is fair if its outputs remain invariant under the counterfactual operation of switching a sensitive attribute, with merit features held fixed. We implement loss based regularization as a symmetry restoring mechanism and evaluate the framework on four synthetic datasets with varying levels of noise, correlation, and bias. The framework achieves upwards of 90\% violation reduction, with accuracy costs around 5\%. This framework does not require causal graph knowledge, is computationally lightweight, and generalizes to any sensitive attribute definable as a bit-flip, making it suitable for contexts where local sources of discrimination remain absent from mainstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [109] [DiBS: Diffusion-Informed Branch Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06518)
*Bo Liu,Yuan Xie,Yuan Gao,Xiaolong Luo,Peng Ye,Tao Chen,Fujun Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiBS 是一种将扩散模型与传统符号求解器结合的数独求解方法，利用扩散模型引导分支选择排序，在保持求解完整性的同时显著降低搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数独求解方法存在互补性局限：基于学习的求解器缺乏硬正确性保证，而完全符号求解器仍易陷入长尾搜索。本文旨在结合两者优势，在保持符号求解器完整性的同时利用学习模型引导搜索过程以降低搜索成本。

Method: 提出 DiBS 方法，核心是将扩散模型作为分支排序引导器嵌入符号求解器。具体地，在给定当前部分赋值和轻量一致性信号下，对候选值进行排序，利用扩散模型指导分支选择搜索过程，同时保持符号求解器的完整性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的 Royle 17-线索数独基准上，DiBS 相比强启发式基线显著降低了搜索成本，尤其在节点数、回溯次数和长尾百分位数方面表现突出。实验证实学习到的全局引导对分支排序错误代价最高的困难实例有效。

Conclusion: DiBS 有效融合了学习方法的全局引导能力与符号方法的完备性保证，为难约束满足问题的求解提供了新范式。提供了理论证明揭示其工作原理。

Abstract: Sudoku is a representative constraint satisfaction problem that requires global structural reasoning under strict discrete constraints. The existing works of solving Sudoku mainly focus on two dominant approaches, i.e., traditional heuristic and deep learning solver. However, they suffer from two complementary limitations: learning-based solvers lack hard correctness guarantees, while complete symbolic solvers are still prone to long-tail search. To address these shortcomings, we propose a novel diffusion model-guided approach, termed as DiBS, for the branch selection search process. Specifically, DiBS keeps the symbolic solver complete and uses the diffusion model as a branch-ordering guide. The core method is ranking candidate values under the current partial assignment and lightweight consistency signal. Furthermore, we provide an in-depth theoretical proof to reveal how it works and why it works. Experiments on the challenging Royle 17-clue Sudoku benchmark show that our DiBS substantially reduces search cost relative to strong heuristic baselines, especially in nodes, backtracks, and long-tail percentiles. Besides, these results confirm that learned global guidance is effective on hard instances where branch-order mistakes are most expensive. All codes are available at https://github.com/shanxierdan/DiBS.

</details>


### [110] [SafeGene: Reusable Adapters for Transferable Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06519)
*Yanghan Wang,Zhiqiang Kou,Fu Feng,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeGene提出了一种可复用的安全适配器模块，通过提取对齐-退化模型差异中的安全表征，实现跨任务的安全能力恢复，在不牺牲下游任务性能的前提下有效降低有害响应率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源LLM在下游微调过程中会弱化安全对齐，即使训练数据本身无害也会导致模型更易受到恶意提示攻击；且模型反复更新时安全问题会反复出现，现有模型特定的安全修复方法效率低下。

Method: SafeGene构建可复用的安全适配器模块：从对齐模型与退化了安全能力的模型之间的差异中提取安全表征；通过数据感知的层选择方法精炼出可跨任务迁移的安全向量；在目标任务模型中通过少量样本的层系数重新校准来注入安全能力。

Result: 在多个模型家族、下游任务和安全评估器上的实验表明，SafeGene增强的模型在保持下游任务性能的同时显著降低了有害响应率，在安全性与效用性的权衡上优于代表性的安全适应方法。

Conclusion: 安全能力可以作为独立于任务更新的可复用适配器表征，通过SafeGene的解耦设计可实现高效的跨任务安全恢复，而无需对每个模型进行单独的修复步骤。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are increasingly fine-tuned into customized assistants, but downstream fine-tuning can weaken safety alignment and make models more vulnerable to malicious prompts, even when the training data is not intentionally harmful. This creates a recurring safety recovery problem as target models are repeatedly updated with new task data or user interactions. We propose SafeGene, a reusable safety-adapter module designed for cross-task reuse within each architecture-compatible model family. Rather than treating safety recovery as a model-specific repair step, SafeGene treats safety capability as an independent, reusable adapter representation decoupled from task-specific updates. This representation is obtained from aligned--degraded model discrepancies, refined into task-transferable safety vectors through data-aware layer selection, and expressed in each downstream task-adapted model via few-shot layer-wise coefficient recalibration. Experiments across multiple model families, downstream tasks, and safety judges show that SafeGene-enhanced models reduce harmful response rates while maintaining downstream performance, outperforming representative safe adaptation methods in safety--utility trade-off.

</details>


### [111] [Lean4Agent: Formal Modeling and Verification for Agent Workflow and Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06523)
*Ruida Wang,Jerry Huang,Pengcheng Wang,Xuanqing Liu,Luyang Kong,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean4Agent是一个利用Lean4依赖类型形式语言对LLM智能体工作流进行形式化建模、验证和调试的框架。它包含FormalAgentLib库（用于形式化建模和验证语义一致性）和LeanEvolve（基于验证结果优化工作流）。实验表明，通过验证的工作流平均性能提升11.94%，LeanEvolve进一步带来7.47%的平均提升。
